		<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content"><div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr"><div role="note" class="hatnote searchaux"> <span typeof="mw:File"><span><img alt="" src="/images/Disambig_color.svg?2db52" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="12" height="9" class="mw-file-element" data-file-width="444" data-file-height="340"></span></span> This article is about the gameplay mechanic.   For the state of blocks and mobs made of copper, see <a href="/w/Weathered" class="mw-redirect" title="Weathered">Weathered</a>.   For the command, see <a href="/w/Commands/weather" title="Commands/weather">Commands/weather</a>.
</div>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/w/File:Weather.png" class="mw-file-description"><img src="/images/thumb/Weather.png/380px-Weather.png?915e7" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="380" height="214" class="mw-file-element" srcset="/images/thumb/Weather.png/760px-Weather.png?915e7 2x" data-file-width="3840" data-file-height="2160"></a><figcaption>The four main weather types, including clear weather, <a href="/w/Rainfall" class="mw-redirect" title="Rainfall">rainfall</a>, <a href="/w/Thunderstorm" title="Thunderstorm">thunderstorm</a>, and <a href="/w/Snowfall" title="Snowfall">snowfall</a></figcaption></figure>
<p><b>Weather</b> in <i><a href="/w/Minecraft" title="Minecraft">Minecraft</a></i> consists of occasional precipitation and storms that are temporary but global.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Behavior">Behavior</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=1&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Behavior" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section's source code: Behavior"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/w/File:WeatherSkyComparison.png" class="mw-file-description"><img src="/images/WeatherSkyComparison.png?d8eb3" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="160" height="480" class="mw-file-element" data-file-width="160" data-file-height="480"></a><figcaption>A comparison of sky colors during different types of weather. In <a href="/w/Bedrock_Edition" title="Bedrock Edition"><i>Bedrock Edition</i></a>, both rainy and stormy weather are grayscale.</figcaption></figure>
<p>The type of weather that occurs depends on several variable factors, including the <a href="/w/Biome#Temperature" title="Biome">temperature</a> of the current biome, and the <a href="/w/Altitude" title="Altitude">altitude</a> in which it occurs. 
</p><p>Players who have cheats enabled can change the current weather if they wish, using the <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/weather" title="Commands/weather">weather</a></span></code> command. In <a href="/w/Bedrock_Edition" title="Bedrock Edition"><i>Bedrock Edition</i></a>, <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/toggledownfall" title="Commands/toggledownfall">toggledownfall</a></span></code> also switches to inclement weather, or vice versa, and <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/weather" title="Commands/weather">weather</a> query</span></code> can be used to determine the current weather.
</p><p>Weather changing with time can be toggled on or off using the "Weather cycle" <a href="/w/Game_settings" class="mw-redirect" title="Game settings">game setting</a>‌<sup class="nowrap Inline-Template" title="">[<i><span title="This statement only applies to Bedrock Edition"><a href="/w/Bedrock_Edition" title="Bedrock Edition">BE</a>  only</span></i>]</sup> or <code>advance_weather</code>/<code>doweathercycle</code> <a href="/w/Game_rule" title="Game rule">game rule</a>. When disabled, the weather stays the same indefinitely, unless altered using the <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/weather" title="Commands/weather">weather</a></span></code> command.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Java_Edition_mechanics"><i>Java Edition</i> mechanics</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=2&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Java Edition mechanics" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section's source code: Java Edition mechanics"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>In <a href="/w/Java_Edition" title="Java Edition"><i>Java Edition</i></a>, weather is controlled by two internal flags that cycle on and off, one flag for rain and one flag for thunder. When the rain flag is on and the thunder flag is off, the weather is rainy (or snowy or overcast, depending on the temperature). When the rain flag is on and the thunder flag is on, the weather is a thunderstorm. When the rain flag is off, the weather is clear, regardless of the thunder flag.
</p><p>Each flag has an associated timer. When the timer reaches zero, the flag is toggled and the timer chooses a new random value. The rain flag will stay on for 12000 to 24000 <a href="/w/Tick" title="Tick">ticks</a> (10 to 20 minutes, inclusive) and off for 12000 to 180000 ticks (10 minutes to 2.5 hours, inclusive). The thunder flag will stay on for 3600 to 15600 ticks (3 to 13 minutes, inclusive) and off for 12000 to 180000 ticks (10 minutes to 2.5 hours, inclusive). The thunder flag only affects the world while the rain flag is on, so it's possible for the thunder flag to cycle on and off while the rain flag stays off, resulting in a longer wait between thunderstorms, or to only partially overlap with a period of rain, resulting in a shorter thunderstorm.
</p><p>There is also a clear weather timer that is only affected by the <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/weather" title="Commands/weather">weather</a> clear</span></code> command and stays at zero during normal gameplay. While this timer is active, the rain and thunder flags are both kept off and their timers are locked at 1 game tick. This means that when the command-enforced clear weather ends and the timers resume, both flags will immediately flip on and both timers will reset, resulting in a thunderstorm.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Effects">Effects</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=3&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Effects" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section's source code: Effects"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Clear weather is the first weather in a newly created world. During clear weather, the <a href="/w/Daylight_cycle" title="Daylight cycle">daylight cycle</a> affects the light level and related effects; no specific effects happen. All celestial bodies are visible as well as regular fog. <a href="/w/Sleeping" class="mw-redirect" title="Sleeping">Sleeping</a> in a bed resets the weather to clear, but not the weather timers.
</p><p>Inclement weather reduces the sky <a href="/w/Light" title="Light">light</a> level, depending on the severity of the storm. Because of this, <a href="/w/Villager" title="Villager">villagers</a> return to their homes until the storm passes.
</p><p>The <a href="/w/Sky" title="Sky">sky</a> itself darkens and gray <a href="/w/Fog" title="Fog">fog</a> increases, to the point where celestial bodies (including the <a href="/w/Sun" title="Sun">sun</a>, <a href="/w/Moon" title="Moon">moon</a>, and <a href="/w/Moon#Stars" title="Moon">stars</a>) are no longer visible, except in dry biomes in <i>Bedrock Edition</i>. The clouds darken depending on the severity of the weather; note that the clouds themselves do not precipitate. Although the sun is not visible during rain, the glow associated with sunrise and sunset is still visible.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Rain">Rain</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=4&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Rain" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section's source code: Rain"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div role="note" class="hatnote searchaux">Main article: <a href="/w/Rain" title="Rain">Rain</a></div>
<p>Rain occurs only in blocks with a temperature higher than 0.15 and not in certain dry biomes (<a href="/w/Deserts" class="mw-redirect" title="Deserts">deserts</a>, <a href="/w/Savannas" class="mw-redirect" title="Savannas">savannas</a>, and <a href="/w/Badlands" title="Badlands">badlands</a>). Rainstorms reduce the light level to 12 in full daylight.
</p><p>Rain extinguishes <a href="/w/Fire" title="Fire">fires</a> (including <a href="/w/Flame" title="Flame">flaming arrows</a>). However, it does not extinguish fires on <a href="/w/Netherrack" title="Netherrack">netherrack</a>, and it does not <a href="/w/Obsidian" title="Obsidian">solidify</a> <a href="/w/Lava" title="Lava">lava</a>. <a href="/w/Mob" title="Mob">Mobs</a> that are on fire are extinguished on contact with rain; this prevents <a href="/w/Skeleton" title="Skeleton">skeletons</a> and <a href="/w/Zombie" title="Zombie">zombies</a> from burning during the day. Conversely, <a href="/w/Endermen" class="mw-redirect" title="Endermen">endermen</a> and <a href="/w/Snow_golem" class="mw-redirect" title="Snow golem">snow golems</a> may die due to their weakness to water. Endermen teleport randomly until they find a dry place. <a href="/w/Blaze" title="Blaze">Blazes</a> and <a href="/w/Strider" title="Strider">striders</a> that are brought into the <a href="/w/Overworld" title="Overworld">Overworld</a> take damage and eventually die from being in contact with rain.
</p>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/w/File:Rainy_Hills.png" class="mw-file-description"><img src="/images/thumb/Rainy_Hills.png/300px-Rainy_Hills.png?8bbfa" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="300" height="169" class="mw-file-element" srcset="/images/thumb/Rainy_Hills.png/600px-Rainy_Hills.png?8bbfa 2x" data-file-width="1366" data-file-height="768"></a><figcaption>A view of hills during rain</figcaption></figure>
<p>Other effects caused by rain:
</p>
<ul><li>While <a href="/w/Fishing" title="Fishing">fishing</a>, there is a shorter wait time on average to get a bite during rain.</li>
<li><a href="/w/Wolves" class="mw-redirect" title="Wolves">Wolves</a> shake themselves dry if they are not moving (certain versions only).</li>
<li><a href="/w/Farmland" title="Farmland">Farmland</a> becomes hydrated if it is not already.</li>
<li>Rain fills up <a href="/w/Cauldron" title="Cauldron">cauldrons</a> with <a href="/w/Water" title="Water">water</a>, albeit slowly.</li>
<li><a href="/w/Trident" title="Trident">Tridents</a> enchanted with <a href="/w/Riptide" title="Riptide">Riptide</a> can be used on land.</li>
<li>Tridents enchanted with <a href="/w/Channeling" title="Channeling">Channeling</a> call down lightning when thrown at a lightning rod in <a href="/w/Bedrock_Edition" title="Bedrock Edition"><i>Bedrock Edition</i></a>, while in <a href="/w/Java_Edition" title="Java Edition"><i>Java Edition</i></a> that requires the occurrence of a <a href="/w/Thunderstorm" title="Thunderstorm">thunderstorm</a>.</li>
<li>Tridents with the <a href="/w/Impaling" title="Impaling">Impaling</a> enchantment deal more damage to entities in rain.‌<sup class="nowrap Inline-Template" title="">[<i><span title="This statement only applies to Bedrock Edition"><a href="/w/Bedrock_Edition" title="Bedrock Edition">Bedrock Edition</a>  only</span></i>]</sup></li>
<li><a href="/w/Villager" title="Villager">Villagers</a> enter houses and stop their daily schedule.‌‌<sup class="nowrap Inline-Template" title="">[<i><span title="This statement only applies to Bedrock Edition"><a href="/w/Bedrock_Edition" title="Bedrock Edition">Bedrock Edition</a>  only</span></i>]</sup></li>
<li><a href="/w/Bee" title="Bee">Bees</a> that are outside return to their hives.</li>
<li>Players are granted the <a href="/w/Conduit_Power" title="Conduit Power">Conduit Power</a> status effect when within a <a href="/w/Conduit" title="Conduit">conduit</a>'s range without having to be in the same body of water.</li>
<li><a href="/w/Axolotl" title="Axolotl">Axolotls</a> and <a href="/w/Dolphin" title="Dolphin">dolphins</a> can survive outside of water.</li></ul>
<p>Rain <a href="/w/Particle" class="mw-redirect" title="Particle">particles</a> make noise when they hit a block, and this noise can be heard at any point within 16 blocks. These particles can be disabled by setting Particles to "Minimal" in the <a href="/w/Options#Video_Settings" title="Options">video settings</a> menu.‌<sup class="nowrap Inline-Template" title="">[<i><span title="This statement only applies to Java Edition"><a href="/w/Java_Edition" title="Java Edition">Java Edition</a>  only</span></i>]</sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Snow">Snow</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=5&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Snow" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section's source code: Snow"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div role="note" class="hatnote searchaux">Main article: <a href="/w/Snowfall" title="Snowfall">Snowfall</a></div><figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/w/File:Snowing_BE.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="/images/thumb/Snowing_BE.jpg/300px-Snowing_BE.jpg?47d6f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="300" height="139" class="mw-file-element" srcset="/images/thumb/Snowing_BE.jpg/600px-Snowing_BE.jpg?47d6f 2x" data-file-width="2684" data-file-height="1241"></a><figcaption>An example of snow falling</figcaption></figure>
<p>Snowfall occurs only in biomes with a base temperature less than 0.15, or above a sufficient altitude. Like rainstorms, snowfall reduces the light level to 12 in full daylight. In <a href="/w/Bedrock_Edition" title="Bedrock Edition"><i>Bedrock Edition</i></a>, <a href="/w/Leaves" title="Leaves">leaves</a> gradually take on a frosted appearance once snowfall begins in snowy biomes.
</p><p>As it snows, <a href="/w/Snow_layer" class="mw-redirect" title="Snow layer">snow layers</a> regenerate over all non-transparent blocks. Snow layers will accumulate and grow over time in <i>Bedrock Edition</i>, or in <a href="/w/Java_Edition" title="Java Edition"><i>Java Edition</i></a> when modifying the <code><span id="max_snow_accumulation_height">max_snow_accumulation_height</span></code> game rule. Snow does not cover <a href="/w/Opacity" title="Opacity">transparent blocks</a>.
</p><p>Effects from rainfall related to the properties of water do not apply with snowfall, for example entities lit on fire do not extinguish. Cauldrons exposed to snowfall slowly fill with <a href="/w/Powder_Snow" title="Powder Snow">powder snow</a>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Thunderstorm">Thunderstorm</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=6&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Thunderstorm" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section's source code: Thunderstorm"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div role="note" class="hatnote searchaux">Main article: <a href="/w/Thunderstorm" title="Thunderstorm">Thunderstorm</a></div>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/w/File:LightningStrike.gif" class="mw-file-description"><img src="/images/thumb/LightningStrike.gif/154px-LightningStrike.gif?51d9a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="154" height="199" class="mw-file-element" srcset="/images/LightningStrike.gif?51d9a 2x" data-file-width="232" data-file-height="299"></a><figcaption>A lightning bolt strikes a <a href="/w/Tree" title="Tree">tree</a>. A creeper can be seen walking through the woods. If it had been closer to the strike, it would have become <a href="/w/Creeper#Charged_creeper" title="Creeper">charged</a>.</figcaption></figure>
<p>Thunderstorms may occur in any biome and are combined with rain, snowfall, or clear weather depending on the temperature. They reduce the <a href="/w/Light#Internal_light_level" title="Light">internal sky light level</a>. At noon the internal sky light level at surface is only 10. However, in mob spawning system, the light level from the sky is treated as if it were 0, allowing hostile mobs to spawn at any time of the day.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Lightning">Lightning</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=7&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Lightning" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section's source code: Lightning"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div role="note" class="hatnote searchaux">Main article: <a href="/w/Thunderstorm#Lightning" title="Thunderstorm">Thunderstorm&nbsp;§&nbsp;Lightning</a></div>
<p><a href="/w/Lightning" class="mw-redirect" title="Lightning">Lightning</a> occurs frequently during thunderstorms, if there is rain. It strikes randomly, creating <a href="/w/Fire" title="Fire">fires</a> where it strikes, igniting any nearby flammable materials, but the rain usually puts the fire out before it can spread. Lightning can strike only blocks that are exposed to the rain. Thunder is a sound that occurs every time lightning strikes, which can be heard at much greater distances than other sounds.
</p><p>Lightning does not occur naturally in biomes that are too hot or dry to have rain or so cold that it snows instead of rains.
</p><p>Lightning deals 5<span class="hidden-alt-text">HP</span><span class="nowrap mc-hearts"><span class="iconbar pixel-image nowrap" style="--icon-offset:-1px;" title="2.5 hearts"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><img alt="❤️" src="/images/Heart_%28icon%29.png?faf83" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="9" height="9" class="mw-file-element" data-file-width="9" data-file-height="9"></span></span><span typeof="mw:File"><span><img alt="❤️" src="/images/Heart_%28icon%29.png?faf83" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="9" height="9" class="mw-file-element" data-file-width="9" data-file-height="9"></span></span><span typeof="mw:File"><span><img alt="💔" src="/images/Half_Heart_%28icon%29.png?cb16e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="9" height="9" class="mw-file-element" data-file-width="9" data-file-height="9"></span></span></span></span> damage to entities on normal difficulty, not including the damage done by the fire it causes. It turns creepers into <a href="/w/Charged_creeper" class="mw-redirect" title="Charged creeper">charged creepers</a>, <a href="/w/Villager" title="Villager">villagers</a> into <a href="/w/Witch" title="Witch">witches</a>, <a href="/w/Pig" title="Pig">pigs</a> into <a href="/w/Zombified_piglin" class="mw-redirect" title="Zombified piglin">zombified piglins</a>, and <a href="/w/Mooshrooms" class="mw-redirect" title="Mooshrooms">mooshrooms</a> into their brown variants. Lightning may also randomly spawn a <a href="/w/Skeleton_Horse" title="Skeleton Horse">"skeleton trap" skeletal horse</a>, depending on the <a href="/w/Difficulty" title="Difficulty">difficulty</a>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="End_flash">End flash</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=8&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: End flash" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section's source code: End flash"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div role="note" class="hatnote searchaux">Main article: <a href="/w/End_sky#End_flash" title="End sky">End sky&nbsp;§&nbsp;End flash</a></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/w/File:Vibrant_Visuals_End_flash.gif" class="mw-file-description"><img src="/images/thumb/Vibrant_Visuals_End_flash.gif/300px-Vibrant_Visuals_End_flash.gif?c9127" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="300" height="169" class="mw-file-element" srcset="/images/Vibrant_Visuals_End_flash.gif?c9127 2x" data-file-width="375" data-file-height="211"></a><figcaption>An End flash with Vibrant Visuals</figcaption></figure>
<p>An End flash is a weather-like event that occurs in the End. With <a href="/w/Vibrant_Visuals" title="Vibrant Visuals">Vibrant Visuals</a>, they act as global directional light sources and illuminate the environment with a purple tint, cast shadows, and create volumetric fog. On classic graphics, they use a pixelated texture and tint block lighting purple.‌<sup class="nowrap Inline-Template" title="">[<i><span title="This statement only applies to Java Edition"><a href="/w/Java_Edition" title="Java Edition">JE</a>  only</span></i>]</sup>
</p><p>There is a 30 second delay between flashes, and each flash lasts between 5 and 19 seconds, occurring at random spots above the horizon, while also producing a sound.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="History">History</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=10&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: History" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section's source code: History"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="msgbox searchaux msgbox-mini msgbox-orange" style="max-width: 50em;">
<div class="msgbox-icon"> <div class="msgbox-image" style=""> <span class="mw-valign-text-top pixel-image" typeof="mw:File"><a href="/w/File:Information_icon.svg" title="File:Information icon.svg"><img alt="" src="/images/Information_icon.svg?15c1c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" class="mw-file-element" data-file-width="62" data-file-height="62"></a></span></div>
</div>
<div style="width: 100%;"><div style="font-weight: bold; display: inline">This section is missing information about: the possibility that rain/snow textures were originally inverted in-game, as shown in the following forum post: <a target="_blank" rel="nofollow noreferrer noopener" class="external free" href="https://www.minecraftforum.net/forums/archive/legacy-support/1841577-weird-rain-bug">https://www.minecraftforum.net/forums/archive/legacy-support/1841577-weird-rain-bug</a> - testing in 22w14a did not reveal any such inversion existing, meaning it was likely fixed at some point between 1.2.5 and 22w14a</div>&nbsp;
<div class="msgbox-text">Please expand the section to include this information. Further details may exist on the <a class="text" href="https://minecraft.wiki/w/Talk:Weather">talk page</a>.</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Java_Edition"><i>Java Edition</i></h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=12&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Java Edition" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section's source code: Java Edition"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r3751077"><table class="wikitable pixel-image" data-description="History"><tbody><tr class="collapsible collapsible-rows"><th class="historytable-subheader" colspan="8" style="border-bottom: none"><span class="collapsetoggle" style="min-width: 40.4688px;">[<span class="jslink" tabindex="0">hide</span>]</span><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Classic" title="Java Edition Classic">Java Edition Classic</a></th></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="1" colspan="7"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Classic_0.26_SURVIVAL_TEST" title="Java Edition Classic 0.26 SURVIVAL TEST">0.26 SURVIVAL TEST</a></th>
<td>Rain was introduced purely for cosmetic purposes, toggled by pressing <kbd class="key nowrap">F5</kbd>. The mechanics were simple, testing only if there is a block above to shelter the rain.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible collapsible-rows"><th class="historytable-subheader" colspan="8" style="border-bottom: none"><span class="collapsetoggle" style="min-width: 40.4688px;">[<span class="jslink" tabindex="0">hide</span>]</span><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Indev" title="Java Edition Indev">Java Edition Indev</a></th></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="1" colspan="7"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Indev_20100218" class="mw-redirect" title="Java Edition Indev 20100218">20100218</a></th>
<td>Pressing <kbd class="key nowrap">F5</kbd> no longer toggles rain.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible collapsible-rows"><th class="historytable-subheader" colspan="8" style="border-bottom: none"><span class="collapsetoggle" style="min-width: 40.4688px;">[<span class="jslink" tabindex="0">hide</span>]</span><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Infdev" title="Java Edition Infdev">Java Edition Infdev</a></th></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="2" colspan="7"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Infdev_20100413-1951" title="Java Edition Infdev 20100413-1951">20100413-1951</a></th>
<td>Pressing <kbd class="key nowrap">F5</kbd> now toggles rain again. The rain effects are broken, resulting in only rain particles appearing around the player. They always appear at sea level.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row">
<td>Since pressing F5 also toggles between first and third person view, these particles are visible only in third person view.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible collapsible-rows"><th class="historytable-subheader" colspan="8" style="border-bottom: none"><span class="collapsetoggle" style="min-width: 40.4688px;">[<span class="jslink" tabindex="0">hide</span>]</span><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Alpha" title="Java Edition Alpha">Java Edition Alpha</a></th></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="1" colspan="7"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Alpha_v1.0.4" title="Java Edition Alpha v1.0.4">v1.0.4</a></th>
<td>Added <a href="/w/Winter_mode" class="mw-redirect" title="Winter mode">Winter mode</a>. While generating a world, there is a 25% chance to get a snowy world, where snow falls continuously. This feature was eventually removed with the introduction of <a href="/w/Biome" title="Biome">biomes</a>.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="1" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Alpha_v1.2.0" title="Java Edition Alpha v1.2.0">v1.2.0</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Category:Information_needed_requiring_unarchived_version" title="Category:Information needed requiring unarchived version">?</a></th>
<td>Removed <a href="/w/Winter_mode" class="mw-redirect" title="Winter mode">Winter mode</a>, and replaced it with <a href="/w/Biome" title="Biome">biomes</a>.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible collapsible-rows"><th class="historytable-subheader" colspan="8" style="border-bottom: none"><span class="collapsetoggle" style="min-width: 40.4688px;">[<span class="jslink" tabindex="0">hide</span>]</span><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Beta" title="Java Edition Beta">Java Edition Beta</a></th></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="4" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Beta_1.5" title="Java Edition Beta 1.5">1.5</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a target="_blank" rel="nofollow noreferrer noopener" class="external text" href="http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qy882ILYJMM">April 8, 2011</a></th>
<td>Weather was shown in a teaser video alongside <a href="/w/Achievement_(Java_Edition)" title="Achievement (Java Edition)">achievements</a>. Thunderstorms are shown to darken the sky/lighting the same amount as rain, despite the video being recorded in singleplayer. Rain and thunder also do not have unique sounds yet, with the former producing the sound of flowing <a href="/w/Water" title="Water">water</a>.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th colspan="4" rowspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Beta_1.5" title="Java Edition Beta 1.5">release</a></th>
<td>Weather was introduced. Rain, snowfall, and thunderstorms were implemented. Rain looks identical to the rain in Classic, but now has unique sounds.
</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row">
<td>Added the ability for lightning strikes to charge creepers and transform pigs into zombie pigmen.
</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row">
<td>Compared to the teaser video, thunderstorms now darken the sky and lighting further than rain, except in multiplayer. Weather in multiplayer also starts and stops instantly with no transitions.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="1" colspan="7"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Beta_1.5_01" title="Java Edition Beta 1.5 01">1.5_01</a></th>
<td>Fixed a bug where rain fell through certain blocks like <a href="/w/Glass" title="Glass">glass</a>, <a href="/w/Stairs" title="Stairs">stairs</a> and <a href="/w/Slab" title="Slab">slabs</a>.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="2" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Beta_1.6" title="Java Edition Beta 1.6">1.6</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="2"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Beta_1.6_Test_Build_3" title="Java Edition Beta 1.6 Test Build 3">Test Build 3</a></th>
<td>Fixed a bug where the rain seemed to freeze when climbing down <a href="/w/Ladder" title="Ladder">ladders</a> until you reached the bottom or changed direction, possibly indicating that you climbed at same speed as the rain fell.
</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row">
<td>Fixed a bug where rain and snow fell underwater.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible collapsible-rows"><th class="historytable-subheader" colspan="8" style="border-bottom: none"><span class="collapsetoggle" style="min-width: 40.4688px;">[<span class="jslink" tabindex="0">hide</span>]</span><a href="/w/Java_Edition_version_history" title="Java Edition version history"><i>Java Edition</i></a></th></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="3" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_1.0.0" title="Java Edition 1.0.0">1.0.0</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Beta_1.9_Prerelease" title="Java Edition Beta 1.9 Prerelease">Beta 1.9 Prerelease</a></th>
<td>Snowfall destroys <a href="/w/Redstone" class="mw-disambig" title="Redstone">redstone</a>, <a href="/w/Torch" title="Torch">torches</a>, <a href="/w/Sapling" title="Sapling">saplings</a>, and minecart <a href="/w/Rail" title="Rail">rails</a>.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th colspan="4" rowspan="2"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_Beta_1.9_Prerelease_5" title="Java Edition Beta 1.9 Prerelease 5">Beta 1.9 Prerelease 5</a></th>
<td>Fixed the above bug.
</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row">
<td>Added the <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/toggledownfall" title="Commands/toggledownfall">toggledownfall</a></span></code> command, which can change the weather.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="2" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_1.3.1" title="Java Edition 1.3.1">1.3.1</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_12w18a" title="Java Edition 12w18a">12w18a</a></th>
<td>As a result of singleplayer being changed to an internal server, weather transitions have been removed, and the sky and lighting during a thunderstorm now look the same as when raining.<sup id="cite_ref-MC-673_1-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MC-673-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_12w23a" title="Java Edition 12w23a">12w23a</a></th>
<td>Weather transitions have been restored.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="1" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_1.4.2" title="Java Edition 1.4.2">1.4.2</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_12w32a" title="Java Edition 12w32a">12w32a</a></th>
<td>Added the <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/weather" title="Commands/weather">weather</a></span></code> command, which can change the weather.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="1" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_1.7.2" title="Java Edition 1.7.2">1.7.2</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_13w39a" title="Java Edition 13w39a">13w39a</a></th>
<td>The clouds, sky, and lighting once again appear darker during thunderstorms.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="2" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_1.8" title="Java Edition 1.8">1.8</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_14w03a" title="Java Edition 14w03a">14w03a</a></th>
<td>Lightning now transforms villagers into witches.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_14w30a" title="Java Edition 14w30a">14w30a</a></th>
<td>Lightning can now be manually summoned with the <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/summon" title="Commands/summon">summon</a> LightningBolt</span></code> command.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="2" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_1.9" title="Java Edition 1.9">1.9</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_15w38a" title="Java Edition 15w38a">15w38a</a></th>
<td>There is a chance (depending on <a href="/w/Regional_difficulty" class="mw-redirect" title="Regional difficulty">regional difficulty</a>) that a lightning strike spawns a <a href="/w/Skeleton_Horse" title="Skeleton Horse">"skeleton trap" skeletal horse</a>.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_16w04a" title="Java Edition 16w04a">16w04a</a></th>
<td>Rain sounds updated. Now more ambient/quieter and sounds more realistic.</td></tr><tr class="collapsible-row"><th class="nowrap" rowspan="1" colspan="3"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_1.11" title="Java Edition 1.11">1.11</a></th><th colspan="4" rowspan="1"><a href="/w/Java_Edition_16w38a" title="Java Edition 16w38a">16w38a</a></th>

<td><a href="/w/Sleeping" class="mw-redirect" title="Sleeping">Sleeping</a> now resets the weather cycle only if it is currently raining or snowing.<sup id="cite_ref-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Therefore, sleeping frequently no longer prevents rain or snow from happening.</td></tr></tbody></table>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Issues">Issues</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=15&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Issues" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section's source code: Issues"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Issues relating to "Weather" are maintained on the <a href="/w/Bug_tracker" title="Bug tracker">bug tracker</a>. Issues should be reported and viewed <a target="_blank" rel="nofollow noreferrer noopener" class="external text" href="https://bugs.mojang.com/issues/?jql=project%20in%20%28MC%2C%20MCPE%29%20AND%20%28resolution%20is%20EMPTY%20OR%20resolution%20in%20%281%2C%202%2C%206%29%29%20AND%20%28summary%20~%20%22Weather%22%29%20ORDER%20BY%20resolution%20DESC">there</a>.
</p>
<div class="issue-list" data-mc="&quot;Confirmation Status&quot; != Unconfirmed AND (resolution is EMPTY OR (resolution = &quot;Fixed&quot; AND affectedVersion = &quot;1.21.10&quot;)) AND (summary ~ &quot;Weather&quot;) ORDER BY resolution ASC, &quot;Mojang Priority&quot; ASC, &quot;Votes Count&quot; DESC, key ASC" data-mcpe="&quot;Confirmation Status&quot; != Unconfirmed AND resolution is EMPTY AND (summary ~ &quot;Weather&quot;) ORDER BY &quot;Votes Count&quot; DESC, key ASC"></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Trivia">Trivia</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/Weather?section=16&amp;veaction=edit" title="Edit section: Trivia" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-divider"> | </span><a href="/w/Weather?action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section's source code: Trivia"><span>edit source</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li>Rain and snow still falls above the clouds. Notch's explanation is that the gray above the clouds during a storm is “another layer of clouds” and the origin of the rain.<sup id="cite_ref-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Rain and snow are both considered the same weather effect by the game. This explains why there is no <code><span>/<a href="/w/Commands/weather" title="Commands/weather">weather</a> snow</span></code> command.</li>
<li>Lightning does not have an image file associated with it. Thus, it is coded directly into the game engine, allowing for dynamic, realistic lightning.</li>
<li><a href="/w/Helen_Zbihlyj" title="Helen Zbihlyj">Helen Zbihlyj</a> created a design document for a scientifically accurate rework of <i><a href="/w/Minecraft" title="Minecraft">Minecraft</a>'</i>s weather system.<sup id="cite_ref-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>

			<div class="thumb" style="width: 150px; height: 150px;"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/w/File:Notch_Weather.jpg" class="mw-file-description" title="An image of snow and rain from Notch's twitter"><img alt="An image of snow and rain from Notch's twitter" src="/images/thumb/Notch_Weather.jpg/120px-Notch_Weather.jpg?9a22c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="120" height="61" class="mw-file-element" srcset="/images/thumb/Notch_Weather.jpg/240px-Notch_Weather.jpg?9a22c 2x" data-file-width="1912" data-file-height="973"></a></span></div>
			<div class="gallerytext">An image of snow and rain from Notch's twitter</div>
		</li>
</ul>

<script>
        document.getElementById('iwb-promo-close').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const cookieExpiry = new Date();
            cookieExpiry.setFullYear(cookieExpiry.getFullYear() + 1);

            document.getElementById('iwb-promo').remove();
            document.cookie = 'iwb_promo=1; path=/; expires=' + cookieExpiry.toString();

        })
</script>
